# Evidence index — 2026-10-17, addenda through 2026-10-26

Single consolidated requirements view: [docs/requirements-evidence-matrix.md](docs/requirements-evidence-matrix.md).

Inspected local retained reports, not a new native execution. Selected file SHA-256
values are in [evidence-hashes.json](docs/handoff/2026-10-17/evidence-hashes.json).
Hashes identify bytes; they do not authenticate outcomes or replace production
seals/protection manifests. Existing attempts remain immutable. This index does
not claim comprehensive historical replay or fresh visual verification.

consumed one-shot attempt and the independent offline rebinding of its retained
bytes (no additional launch).

**The same addendum, fourth entry:** E28 records the operator-authorized
one-shot native run of `D4-CORRIDOR-3` and its close-out. The candidate was

`PARTIAL_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE`, `verified: false`, `status: FAILED_CLEANUP`;
`after-diagnostic.sav` (`b52d0ecf…970b`) is not an authoritative save, no
`after.sav` or `receipt.json` exists, and the read-only replay refuses with

state 5 rather than `TIME_WAIT`, so the workspace and isolated credential were
not disposed). The candidate manifest re-verifies 48/48. No retry, re-sign,
replacement candidate or claim reuse occurred; **Milestone D4 remains
INCOMPLETE (native)** and any future attempt needs a fresh separately
authorized candidate with the `op-03` admission defect corrected.

running cost bills at every charge event of the E113 event lattice of record (the `T0+1+74k` spread slots, the 8.8 fixed-point carry, the uint8-wrap borrow kernel unchanged); (D2) income measured, never modeled — engine readback at the same observation instants, per-span attribution; (D3) OPEX = OBSERVED native cash deltas only, with the four label classes of record spelled — OBSERVED (retained receipt reads), DERIVED (any arithmetic on observed/derived inputs; labeled, cited, recomputable, never the truth class), CONTROLLED (pinned inputs the contract controls — world pins, budgets, schedules, seeded constants; cited to their pins; never evidence that the world did anything), UNKNOWN (unestablished facts; never reclassified, never filled); (D4) unexplained movement stays UNKNOWN and closes by the fence (divergence → halt), never by the taxonomy; (D5) revenue never profit (margins DERIVED with inputs cited; the round-6/round-7 COMPLETE-not-delivered terminals reported as observed); (D6) the zero-residual fence carries into every replay (a replay failure reports a finding — it never normalizes, rounds or absorbs the residual); (D7) reporting rules — identity attribution travels with every number, labels travel with the numbers they qualify, milestone rates are script-tick rates, receipts replay into fresh sibling outputs with no historical output overwritten. **Rejected alternatives recorded with reasons**: calendar-day OPEX bucketing (contradicts the E113 event law;
